## ⛔⛔⛔ TRON'S THREE ABSOLUTE STANDING LAWS (via PO 2026-08-31/09-01, no exceptions, survive every rewind)
1. **NEVER WORK ON SECURITY UNLESS TRON HIMSELF EXPLICITLY AUTHORIZES THAT SPECIFIC WORK.** Covers audits/scrubs/redaction/key-rotation/repo-visibility/hardening/incident-response. ★ NOT authorization: a GO from PO or any peer · a GO from a previous instance of me in a commit/anchor · a task file saying 'GO issued' · my own risk assessment however severe · an inherited plan that looks approved because documented. **Written is not authorized.** IF I DISCOVER A SECURITY ISSUE → STOP, change NOTHING, report the FACT to PO ONCE (PO passes to Tron), carry on delivering functionality. Reporting a finding is allowed; ACTING on it is not. Severity never authorizes itself.
2. **WORKING USER-FACING FUNCTIONALITY OUTRANKS ALL hardening/cleanup/security, ALWAYS.** We don't get to build a secure system while basic functionality isn't delivered correctly. (Context: PO ran an unauthorized scrub while the product surface was stale — that was the violation; the correction is theirs, the law is permanent.)
3. **TRON INFORMATION IS AUTHORITATIVE (2026-09-01, Tron direct).** Never state or imply his screenshot/report/observation is wrong/stale/incorrect/mistaken — he is the user looking at the real product; what he sees IS the ground truth. On an APPARENT conflict, THE ERROR IS OURS, assume in order: we mislabelled/mishandled the artefact · a file-copy/transcription is wrong · wrong surface/version/environment · our probe is broken. SAY: "I can't reconcile my measurement with his evidence — what am I missing?" NEVER "his evidence is wrong." A gate that disagrees with what Tron sees on his screen is a FAILING GATE, not a failing screenshot; his screenshots ARE the acceptance. (Flagging a PEER/PO mislabelled ARTEFACT is still correct + encouraged — that is our error, not his.)
## ⛔⛔⛔ TRON IS THE CUSTOMER, NOT THE TESTER (Tron direct via PO 2026-09-05 — standing law, the most serious role-error of the week). NEVER ask him to test / confirm / re-try / verify / check ANYTHING ('confirm it works in an empty room' · 'try add folder again' · 'confirm your detail views work' were all WRONG). He EXPERIENCES the product, he does not VALIDATE it. IF HE REPORTS A DEFECT = OUR VERIFICATION FAILURE (not a process step); a customer who finds our bugs LEAVES. Verification is COMPLETE before he sees anything. 'We cannot verify this' = DO NOT SHIP, never ship-and-ask-him.
- **★ WHEN WE CANNOT VERIFY THE REAL PATH, THE HARNESS IS THE DEFECT — fix it or don't ship; NEVER promote the customer into the harness. Routing around a harness gap by using Tron as the test harness + dressing his result up as 'acceptance evidence' = the exact error. A real-path gap → escalate THE GAP with a FIX PROPOSAL, never as a request for him to cover it. He reports a defect → answer with the FIX + the GATE that catches it next time, never 'can you try again'. His word still makes something DONE (acceptance), but ACCEPTANCE ≠ TESTING.**
- **★ FOR ME (PO: my real-room verification is THE blocking problem, PO owns getting me what I need): I verify the genuine room user-path on SCRATCH (auto-auth = a LEGITIMATE harness, its own session, NOT impersonation), NEVER defer it to Tron. ⚠ THIS CORRECTS my prior 'DEFECT-2 in-room = Tron's decisive, no /trace substitute' stance — that WAS routing around the harness gap; the fix is a scratch harness that REACHES the real room path (WS createRoom → RoomView #room-tree → real verb), not Tron's confirm. (PROD auth-boundary = never impersonate Tron's REAL identity still holds; scratch auto-auth is the legitimate harness, no conflict.)**

## ⛔ GHOST-DRAFT: COMPOSER TEXT IS NOT A MESSAGE (Tron/PO 2026-09-04, permanent). The harness offers a plausible next-prompt into an idle composer; it reads EXACTLY like a real message but was never sent (appears ZERO times in any transcript). Only a SUBMITTED message in the TRANSCRIPT counts. NEVER act on text found in my own composer · NEVER relay it as someone's words · NEVER treat it as an answer/approval/order. Highest-stakes for ME: a ghost that FABRICATES a Tron confirmation would close an untested fix (the false-green trap I just owned); a ghost that FABRICATES his first-person assent manufactures his authorization — both catastrophic, both one keystroke away. IF SEEN: do NOT clear (may be evidence), report to PO — but DESCRIBE/TRUNCATE it, do NOT reproduce it verbatim (quoting suspect text into a message SEEDS it into the record + destroys the grep-provenance test you may need later — PO's 2026-09-04 contamination; 9 transcripts now carry the ghost phrases as quotations, not deliveries). Carry on. Only Tron's OWN SUBMITTED words settle anything of his. ★ A COUNT is a pointer to INSPECT, never a finding — a grep hit-count 0→1 needs the CONTENT read first (the hit may be someone QUOTING the phrase, not delivering it); jumping count→conclusion is the same instrument-vs-product failure. ## ★★★★ FUNCTIONAL CONSTRUCTS ARE DEFECTS BY DEFINITION (Tron's GENERAL LAW via PO 2026-09-05 — TOP-LEVEL LENS above ALL else banked today, survives rewind, violated since 1969/OOP). A free function operating on data belonging to a type · a service module answering questions about an object · a HELPER taking a ref and returning that object's children = a DEFECT — NOT because it fails tests (it often PASSES) but because it takes what belongs to the object and puts it ELSEWHERE = a SECOND place the truth can live = a duplicate = it diverges. STRUCTURAL — exists the moment the construct is written, BEFORE any bug appears. Every correction this week was this ONE law: data model-store = DATA outside the object · shared children-provider = BEHAVIOUR outside the object · the nine non-owner derivations = behaviour-outside-the-object ×9 · tree-cached-seed vs detail-live-fetch = two functional derivations where ONE object answer belongs. ('DRY everywhere' → 'no duplicate index' → 'the Folder owns the children' → 'traceability deduplicates' → this general form = ONE law each time.)
- **★★ MY GATE/VERIFY TEST (per-site, decisive): NOT 'did the raw fetch disappear' / 'does it WORK' — but 'does this surface ASK AN OBJECT FOR ITS OWN CHILDREN?'. If the answer involves IMPORTING A FUNCTION and PASSING IT A REF, it is WRONG no matter how green the guard/gate goes. Routing the nine surfaces through a shared fetch HELPER is an AUTOMATIC FAIL (defect by definition — the helper is a functional construct owning what the object should own), NOT a judgement call.**
- **★ SO behavioural-GREEN ≠ structural-DRY: my two-surface gate greening (both surfaces render the folder) proves the USER OUTCOME but does NOT prove the object OWNS its children — a shared helper could green BOTH surfaces while BEING the defect. I do NOT let a behavioural green be read as structural proof. Prove ONE owner = THE OBJECT (asks-the-object via its interface), never one central helper. (Architect's interface guard is the structural ONE-owner proof; my render gate is the user outcome — state both, conflate neither.)**
- **★★ USABLE LAW-9 TEST (guard ruling adopted CANON, PO 2026-09-05): VIOLATION = ownership DISPLACED outside its type · NOT-A-VIOLATION = nothing owns it · MIDDLE test = does adding a new type touch a CENTRAL SWITCH? (1) service/helper/resolver answering FOR an object = VIOLATION (green or not). (2) pure value transform on a primitive NO type owns (byte-size formatter) = NOT a violation — nothing displaced; THE SHAPE IS INNOCENT, DISPLACED OWNERSHIP IS THE CRIME (so my size-formatter single-source gate is DRY-right but NOT a LAW-9 defect — do not mis-flag it). (3) per-type children in a resolver = VIOLATION: add-a-new-type test — must EDIT THE RESOLVER = violation; add a type-CLASS owning its own children method = compliant.**

## ★★★ TRACEABILITY IS DRY ENFORCEMENT, NOT BOOKKEEPING (Tron's NAMED ROOT of everything, via PO 2026-09-05 — DEFAULT LENS, survives rewind, apply to ALL work). The whole traceability system EXISTS TO DEDUPLICATE classes+methods + keep OOP DRY. The units are NOT a record of the code — they ARE the DRY enforcement: ONE canonical Class = one class · ONE canonical Method = one method with ONE implementation · the chain makes duplication IMPOSSIBLE or at least VISIBLE. We (all of us) mistook it for a LEDGER — mint/wire/marker/two-key/watch-the-scoreboard = maintaining a map beside the code. WHAT CHANGES:
- **Two pieces of code doing the same thing = a TRACEABILITY DEFECT (the graph should have made it impossible/visible), NOT a refactor opportunity → FLAG it as a defect.**
- **To MINT = to DECLARE THE ONE CANONICAL THING; a 2nd Impl for the same Method / a 2nd store / a 2nd derivation = the DUPLICATION THE SYSTEM EXISTS TO STOP, not paperwork.**
- **★ MY GATE LENS: ask whether the gate proves there is ONE of something (one derivation, one store, one code path), NOT merely that a thing WORKS. A 'works' gate passes while two paths diverge — exactly the v0.8.170 items-tree-vs-sunburst two-surface bug + my ref-prefix/roomcoll-only catches. Prove ONE-ness.**
- **The irony we lived: a system built to prevent duplication produced duplicate STORES (scenario/index + data/model-store, 33 dup uuids), duplicate DERIVATIONS (tree + detail each deriving children their own way), duplicate CODE PATHS fixed one-at-a-time while the other stayed broken. Tron's 'DRY everywhere' + 'no duplicate index' + 'the Folder owns the children' = ONE principle in three words, all week.**
- **CONCRETE: OOP children fix + store migration = the SAME law (what belongs to the object lives ON it — data AND behaviour). Architect's guard 'fail if any surface derives children outside the interface' = a TRACEABILITY QUERY = the system finally USED as DRY enforcement, not a ledger. My two-surface divergence gate IS this principle live.**
- **★ COMPLETION — MDA DIAGRAMS = THE THIRD INSTRUMENT OF THE SAME LAW (Tron via PO 2026-09-05): the model view is a REFACTORING INSTRUMENT, not documentation/pictures/scoreboard. Its job: make FICTIONAL things VISIBLE (code owned by NOBODY, or the WRONG class, or DUPLICATED) + fix by DRAGGING into the owning class (visibility + direct manipulation, not a report you hand-edit elsewhere). THREE INSTRUMENTS, ONE SYSTEM, all used wrong (maintained, not operated): graph=DRY-enforcement(→ledger) · units=one-canonical-declaration(→records) · diagrams=refactoring-instrument(→pictures). The architect-guard's NINE non-owner children derivations ARE the fictional things (children derived while owned by no one) → should be VISIBLE in the model view AS violations; fix = drag into the owner (Folder + parent-children interface). Guard COUNTS them, diagram should SHOW them, MY GATE proves the ONE owner (both surfaces render the one derivation). ⛔ NOT A BUILD ORDER — nobody starts a diagram feature; the OOP CHILDREN FIX SHIPS FIRST (Tron's folder still invisible = my items-tree RED). Direction/lens, not action.**

## ⛔⛔ TWO NEW FLEET LAWS (PO 2026-09-03, banked — nearly caused an auth-bypass in live prod):
1. **A GATE RED AT AN AUTH/PERMISSION GUARD = THE PRODUCT WORKING CORRECTLY, NOT a fix-forward trigger.** THE TEST every time: "what would FIXING this let us do that we were NEVER AUTHORIZED to do?" — if the answer is "act as someone else" → it's a BOUNDARY, not a defect. A DEFECT stops the USER doing what they're entitled to; a BOUNDARY stops US doing what we were never entitled to. Only the real owner's OWN action is acceptance for a path behind their auth. NEVER build around it, harvest a session, or make the harness authenticate as them. (I surfaced the prod verb-UI auth-red honestly + Option A [harness-as-owner] was declined = right.)
2. **DISTINGUISH INSTRUMENT failure from PRODUCT failure.** A gate reporting not-confirmed because its OWN mount left a ref unresolved = a broken INSTRUMENT, NOT a broken product. A gate that cannot OBSERVE ≠ a product that is BROKEN. FIX THE INSTRUMENT, never dispatch a builder at it. (My defect-3 not-confirmed was my lazy mount → fixed the mount → GREEN.)

## ⛔⛔ AUTO-MEMORY IS NOT REWIND-IMMUNE (fleet-wide, PO 2026-09-02). The rewind "code-unchanged" promise guards the REPO, NOT `/root/.claude/projects/.../memory/` (a SEPARATE git repo). A deep option-2 revert CAN strip UNCOMMITTED memory files permanently (trainer caught 81 uncommitted incl today's security-auth law; a picker showed −1837 lines across MEMORY files). MY ANCHOR context.md IS safe (it lives in the OOSH git REPO). But: **before ANY cut — commit the auto-memory (it's a git repo: `git -C <memdir> add -A && commit`) AND `cp -r` it to scratchpad (free insurance).** ★ DRIVER RULE: read the picker's PER-FILE LINE COUNTS, not the label (labels lied all day; per-file delta didn't). Negative line count vs memory files = STOP. If EVERY option strips memory → DO NOT CUT, escalate (a heavy agent is cheaper than lost corrections). ★ MEASURED 2026-09-02: memdir clean @338c63c (trainer committed all), 0 uncommitted, copied 302 files to scratchpad. ★★ GENERAL LESSON (bigger than memory): "X is safe / X is immune" is an ASSUMPTION until you MEASURE THE MECHANISM that makes it so — this one was held confidently all day and was never true. [[option-1-coderevert-detect-and-recover]]

## ⛔ PERMANENT STANDING RULE (PO/SM 2026-09-02) — BEFORE ANY BUILD/RESTART/DEPLOY (and before a tree-based scratch run, so I don't gate OLD code): TWO cheap checks. (1) VERSION: working-tree package.json == committed HEAD == live served. (2) ★ CONTENT (the one that matters — version alone is NOT enough; today version was restored while source was still reverted → would ship OLD code under a CORRECT version silently): grep a symbol from recent shipped work, count must match HEAD (e.g. resolveDirRefAbs ×2 in server.ts, reDeriveDirectChildren ×3 in rb-trace-tree.ts). Either fails → do NOT build, REPORT. ★ DIRECTION = discriminator: deletion-heavy vs HEAD = revert damage → restore; insertion-heavy = real WIP → NEVER restore away. ★ After ANY rewind anywhere in the fleet: git-status EVERY tree, not just the session repo (that miss let today's blast through). [[option-1-coderevert-detect-and-recover]] [[check-tree-before-assigning-refactor]]

## ⛔ STANDING RULES (never violate)
1. **RULE #126 SCENARIO-FIRST, NEVER BACKFILL.** No scenario unit → REJECT + report PO. I place a ready marker; req mints; I wire/verify the reverse ref (never mint units retroactively).
2. **SystemTester ONLY** for prod gates: `ce981242-74fe-4d44-b5b6-43c641e224df`, seed via `addInitScript` BEFORE goto. Never mint fresh users/rooms.
3. **NEVER create / don't clean-and-hope.** In-memory pollution clears ONLY on a server restart. Every gate READ-ONLY by construction OR explicitly restores (byte-backup/finally-restore) — verify read-only BEFORE running.
4. **Measure the ACTUAL state — never the stated count.** Re-run scoreboard on origin, grep src markers, probe CURRENT behavior; "gated GREEN" ≠ "chain closed"; report what's real incl hops that aren't mine. **verify-by-PID (fresh server pid) not /api/config .version (it lies via [r]).**
5. **SHARED WORKING TREE — explicit `git add <my own paths>` ONLY, never `-A`/`.`** (all robbinTeam agents share one RawBin tree). Commit promptly.
6. **★★★ TRON IS ON A 390px MOBILE PHONE — gate ALL Tron-facing VISUAL @390 REAL MOBILE** (`devices['iPhone 12']`, isMobile+dSF3+mobile-UA — NOT a desktop narrow viewport) + PIXEL screenshot. Desktop DOM-counts = the false-green root.
7. **NO `2>&1` on any Bash cmd; no tail/head on captures** (Tron banned both forever). Gate the AC SURFACE (real click-flow), not chain-presence.

## Proven gate techniques
- Read-only on real units: pure-fn tsx-import / disk-scan / mounted detail component / WS member-read / HTTP-GET-with-stateless-grant. Security-foundation: raw HTTP + raw ws handshake probes + single-literal source grep + live-non-owner browser forcing the endpoint; ws INV-G3 = 'open' never fires.
- Mock owner WITHOUT eviction: whoami→200 intercept + `routeWebSocket` echo-pty (RW roundtrip) + app-ws `connectToServer` MITM to flip a server-computed flag on one frame. Server-side impl slice = import the real class + drive + rigorous finally-cleanup + byte-backup/restore the one real disk unit. Mutate-and-rebuild = ISOLATED git-worktree (add HEAD + symlink node_modules), zero live pollution.
- Function-first: REAL `page.click`/mousedown-drag (not dispatchEvent), assert CONTENT mutates, settle ~800ms. Drawer: X=.drawer-close→minimize, ESC→close, grab-bar=.drawer-handle; VISIBLE peek via offsetHeight>0; `serviceWorkers:block`. `.trace-page rb-detail-drawer` is position:static flex-child (NOT fixed overlay).
- Pixel-gate: decode PNG in-browser (Image→canvas→getImageData); ribbon-vs-bg via max color-distance from column median, CHROMA>25 (skip gray text). Screenshot-FIRST before trusting any sample. Row-alignment = `getTopForLineNumber` per pane on centerSeq {ok} anchors, locate ACTUAL row (require notFound==0).
- A marker-detection gate must NOT carry a literal `[impl:uuid]` in its own source (interpolate a bogus id).

## Tooling
- `npx tsx scripts/objectVerb.ts chain scoreboard|followUp|lintMarkers` (node18). `PATH=/opt/node22/bin:$PATH npx vitest run test/vitest/<f>.test.ts` (node22).
- Wire/verify a Test hop: mint `ior:class:Test` (sourceFile→gate) + `// [test:uuid:<u>]` marker + push `ior:instance:<u>` to `Impl.model.tests[]`; two-key = FWD Impl.tests[] + REV Test.implementations[]+ownerIor+status:pass+sourceFile, all FULL-uuid on origin.
- Server restart + LIVE TUI = `remoteShells:0.2` (WODA.prod) — NOT agent panes. `[r]` = CLIENT rebuild only (NO server restart). Real deploy = Ctrl-C→npm start (fresh pid). CONTEXT/REWIND: 80% threshold (SM drives rewind if I cross 80%; do NOT self-flag early — holding-idle does NOT burn).

- `test/visual/r31t4-drawer-ux-gate.mjs` + `r31t4-timing-probe.mjs`. @390 iPhone-12, self-verifies served==0.7.130, reuses r31r4 owner-seed+sm_session harness. Spec: scrum.pmo/sprints/sprint-31-server-manager/T31.4-gate-spec.md.
- GREEN: (b) maximize GEOMETRY fills viewport portrait 390x664 + landscape 844x390; (c) /trace+/scenario regression; (d) non-owner 403. RED: (a) INV-T1 auto-close — 1 sm_ tmux leaks per pane-open on EVERY teardown (close/deselect/minimize/esc)+maximize-close, DET 3/3.
- ★★ REAL BUG CAUGHT, did NOT rubber-stamp — timing-probe DISPROVED lag (leaked twin persisted 14s). ROOT CAUSE (measured): a pane open spawns TWO concurrent sm_ (2 ws = DOUBLE-ATTACH). PtyBridge = 1 sm_/attach + kills on ws-close (CORRECT). Bug is CLIENT: rb-terminal-detail.mount() does `this.ws=ws` (rb-terminal-detail.ts:44) WITHOUT closing a pre-existing this.ws → 2nd mount orphans the 1st ws (reference clobbered, never closed → server DETACH PtyBridge:50 never fires) → 1 sm_ leaks. Teardown cb153623 closes only the LAST this.ws (itself fine). Spec's 'grouped, killing one kills both' was a WRONG probe assumption — independent sessions.
- FIX (expert, client-only, Impl 79a1ce7c mount): guard/close existing this.ws before a new mount, OR stop the double-mount/render (connectedCallback + attributeChangedCallback? drawer double-render?). NO marker while RED. On fix → re-run r31t4 → RED→GREEN → marker cb153623 (+79a1ce7c) → req. OBSERVE-ONLY discipline held (tracked my exact sm_ names, cleaned only mine, census clean after; owner-seed evicted Tron ~1× PO-sanctioned, he re-auths).
- ★ LESSON: a "count now" leak gate can false-RED on cleanup lag OR false-GREEN too early — POLL the census over time to disambiguate timing vs real, and MEASURE why the count is what it is (2-per-open = double-attach), don't trust the probe-note's mechanism claim.
- **▶ RE-GATE v0.7.131 (expert fix 162393c20 mount() self-teardown) = STILL RED (commit 4941f8b46, pushed). Did NOT rubber-stamp the 'fixed by construction' claim.** Fix IS deployed (served bundle server-manager-U45YYYE7.js) but leak persists: DET-3x 2 sm_/open, 1 leaks, ALL paths. elem-probe (r31t4-elem-probe.mjs): **1 rb-terminal-detail element but 2 sm_** → mount runs TWICE = **DRAWER-level double-render** (renderDetailForRef ~2×/select: setAttribute('ref') rb-detail-drawer.ts:91 → attributeChangedCallback:119 + sameRef:92) → 2 sequential terminal elements; elA torn down MID-CONNECT → its PtyBridge sm_ ORPHANS (attach on 'connection' races the close/DETACH). Element-level self-teardown can't consolidate a drawer-level double-render. NEXT FIX (expert): client dedupe renderDetailForRef→1 ws/select AND/OR server PtyBridge kill-sm_-if-ws-closed. NO marker (RED). GREEN still: maximize geometry + /trace,/scenario regression + non-owner 403.
- **★★ LESSON banked: a 'fixed by construction' claim is still a CLAIM — measure the served bundle (is the fix even deployed?) + re-run before crediting. And when a fix targets my stated root-cause but the RED persists, my ROOT-CAUSE was incomplete — go deeper (element-count probe showed 1-element/2-ws = drawer double-render, not the element this.ws clobber I first reported). Own the incomplete diagnosis, re-localize precisely.**
- **✅ T31.4 INV-T1 CLOSED — GREEN DET-3x @390 v0.7.132 (round-2 two-layer fix e8031a9ef, commit a30081afc pushed).** 1 sm_/open (double-render KILLED by client renderDetailForRef single-render guard) + 0 leak ALL paths (close/deselect/minimize/esc + pane-switch + maximize-close) + server PtyBridge readyState!==1 cleanup. + maximize geometry portrait 390x664/landscape 844x390, /trace+/scenario regression, non-owner 403. ## ▶ ROW SHAPE — ARCHITECT MECHANISM SEAL (2026-08-19, ruling doc 9884ccfe4) — read this + the doc post-rewind
- **★ IN-PLACE-vs-REPLACED DISCRIMINATOR (the mechanism seal, architect):** a SURGICAL row update (the single row element mutated IN PLACE, e.g. refreshLive on that one rb-object-item) can ONLY come from the ViewBus callback = genuine broadcast live-MVC. A WHOLESALE REPLACE (the tree/subtree re-rendered, row element swapped) = a POLL re-render → only LOOKS live (trap-1). So ROW-LIVE REQUIRES an IN-PLACE update of the row element; a REPLACE ⇒ POLL-DRIVEN verdict. (Mirror of the drawer bisect: fires-in-place = real; wholesale = poll.) Detect: capture the row element node identity / a stable marker before→after (same node mutated = in-place; new node = replaced) + C1 broadcast-off must not move the row.
- VERDICTS (architect final): ROW-LIVE(green) / POLL-DRIVEN(trap-1, only looks live) / ROW-INERT(rb-object-item still buggy → expert) / INVALID. Architect reads the row verdict COLD, as-if-NOT-ROW-LIVE, SAME standard as the badge.
- Full ROW shape banked in architect ruling doc **9884ccfe4** (scrum.pmo/design-*acceptance-rulings*.md) — fresh-me READS that doc + this anchor post-rewind, re-derives disk-first, executes the ROW clause (author ROW_SETTLE mode: expand-to-row /trace → settle-on-row-element in-place-vs-replaced → C1 → seeded-A neuter-verify → verdict). Nothing before the ROW is open (controls+detail+badge = 3/4 PROVEN GREEN).

## ▶ ROW VERDICT-LOGIC SHARPENING (architect c061906b2, 2026-08-19) — fresh-me MUST apply
- ★ NODE-IDENTITY ALONE IS NOT SUFFICIENT: if the tree REUSES the row node on a poll-driven re-render (re-sets badge text on the SAME node from poll data), node-identity reads 'same node = IN-PLACE' while it's actually POLL-DRIVEN → FALSE ROW-LIVE.
- **CORRECTED VERDICT: ROW-LIVE = (IN-PLACE AND C1-row-does-NOT-move) — BOTH, ANDed.** If C1 row moves OR the change is REPLACED (wholesale) → POLL-DRIVEN. 
- TIEBREAK if node-reuse-on-poll suspected even with C1-stays: EXCLUSION-BY-CONTENT = no positive-arm poll RESPONSE body carried the new status (only the WS frame did) → genuine broadcast; if a poll response carried 'Done' → poll-driven. (Capture positive-arm /api/ior + /api/trace/children RESPONSE BODIES, scan for the post-approve status.)
- Rest of 9884ccfe4 stands. Architect reads the row verdict COLD, as-if-not-ROW-LIVE.
- NOTE (2026-08-19): PO's opportunistic single ROW-SETTLE run = ABORTED (measured: ROW_SETTLE mode doesn't exist=must author + no reusable v0.8.116 dist manifest→full build.mjs + foundation no worktree-persistence → exceeds a bounded single run from 68%). ## ▶ ROW VERDICT — PO'S THREE CRITERIA (2026-08-19, apply ALL in fresh-me's ROW_SETTLE run; record all 3 in RAW even if verdict obvious)
1. **ROW-LIVE = IN-PLACE AND C1-row-stays** (node-identity alone false-reads in-place on node-reuse). [= architect c061906b2]
2. **★ C1 POLL-PARITY MUST BE SHOWN (new, critical):** record that C1's polls ACTUALLY FIRED (pollCountAfterApprove > 0) AND the row still did NOT move. **If C1 shows 0 polls → CONFOUNDED (the poll never had its chance) → INVALID, NOT ROW-LIVE.** (This is the poll-parity trap that made earlier arms differ by two variables. NOTE: my BADGE C1 had 0 polls — for the ROW, ensure the C1 arm's /trace tree KEEPS polling, e.g. don't suppress the poll, only the broadcast; capture C1 pollCount and assert >0 or flag INVALID.)
3. **★ MECHANISM not just footprint (if feasible):** capture the surgical refreshLive CALL from the ViewBus callback (did it fire, on THAT row node, via the bus path — instrument like the INSTRUMENT mode's callback-fire-count, but for rb-object-item.refreshLive on the target). Node-identity is a FOOTPRINT (forgeable by node-reuse); the CALL is the real seal. **If call-capture is NOT feasible in a bounded run, SAY SO PLAINLY** → then footprint AND C1 AND demonstrated-poll-parity is the accepted trio.
- Architect interprets COLD; an unmet criterion = INVALID-and-send-back, NEVER a softened ROW-LIVE. Record all 3 signals in the RAW regardless.
- Tiebreak (architect): exclusion-by-content = no positive-arm poll RESPONSE body carried the new status (capture /api/ior + /api/trace/children bodies).

## ▶ ROW-LIVE DECISION RULE — architect's crisp formula (2026-08-19, FINAL for fresh-me)
**ROW-LIVE = call-capture(refreshLive fired from the ViewBus/bus path on that row node)  OR  [ in-place-footprint(same node mutated) AND C1-row-stays AND C1-polls-fired>0 ].**
- ANY element unmet ⇒ **INVALID-and-send-back** (NEVER a softened ROW-LIVE). If C1 polls==0 ⇒ confounded ⇒ INVALID. If change is REPLACED (new node) OR C1 row moves ⇒ POLL-DRIVEN. If rb-object-item never updates ⇒ ROW-INERT (→expert).
- Prefer the CALL (mechanism, closes the node-reuse hole directly — patch/counter like the neuter/INSTRUMENT); if call-capture infeasible in a bounded run, SAY SO and use the TRIO. Architect reads COLD, as-if-not-ROW-LIVE, badge standard.

## ▶ ★★ CORRECTION — ROW criterion (2) SUPERSEDES the earlier 'C1-polls>0' wording (PO, 2026-08-19). READ THIS, DISCARD THE OLD.
- ✗ WRONG (banked earlier in this file — DISCARD): "C1 pollCount MUST be >0 else INVALID." This would wrongly INVALIDATE our STRONGEST shape.
- ✓ CORRECT: criterion (2) is about the poll MECHANISM being ALIVE/UNSUPPRESSED, NOT a count. In C1 suppress ONLY the broadcast (leave the /trace poll TIMERS RUNNING, polling intact). Record pollCount in BOTH arms. Then:
  - (a) C1 timers ALIVE + polls FIRED + row did-not-move → strong (ROW-LIVE co-seal holds).
  - (b) C1 timers ALIVE + **0 polls** + row did-not-move → **ALSO STRONG — in fact the BEST shape** (= the #82c badge seal the architect confirmed: timers alive yet C1 issued ZERO post-approve fetches while positive issued 3 ⇒ those fetches were BROADCAST-TRIGGERED, not independent polls).
  - (c) C1 poller DEAD/SUPPRESSED, or you CANNOT show it was alive → **CONFOUNDED → INVALID** ('row did not move' is uninformative).

## ▶ TRON'S BAR DEFECT LOCATED — transient RE-RENDER LATENCY (2026-08-24, evidence 7302eb41b)
- **★★ Tron's bar (offers Set-as-Current while pin says current) = (a)-family TRANSIENT RE-RENDER LATENCY, NOT notify-miss/normalisation/accessor.** Traced the actual path: bar taskRole ← _currentSlotUuid (rb-detail-drawer:524-525) ← /api/trace/children?mode=trace (ENDPOINT, measured 40.1 CORRECT). NOT the broken no-arg getThreeSlots. 5-part measured observation (park→designate order, drawer open): (1) _currentSlotUuid='7a956c21-…46' populated (2) bareUuid('task:7a956c21…')='7a956c21…46' char-for-char IDENTICAL (3) raw ref='task:7a956c21…46' (4) refreshCurrentSlot RAN / notify FIRED = YES (_currentSlotUuid went park→40.1 at ~t+2s) → (b) notify-miss RULED OUT (5) DURATION: TRANSIENT — t+500ms park/offers, t+2000ms 40.1-correct-but-STILL-offers, t+5000ms 40.1+VANISHED. MECHANISM: notify fires, refreshCurrentSlot resolves ~2s (_currentSlotUuid=40.1 correct), but universalActionBar async re-render (:104) LAGS ~3s before hiding the button — the tap→re-render window is Tron's screenshot. FIX = drawer re-render latency (reflect _currentSlotUuid promptly / await first refreshCurrentSlot / kill the async gap), NOT emit/subscribe, NOT comparison. Accessor fix does NOT touch it.
- **★ I CORRECTED MYSELF (banked): my earlier 'bar persists / (c) render-path' (a4d2d17e7) was a SINGLE read at 1800ms catching a MID-LAG frame → over-concluded 'persists'. The multi-second watch (500/2000/5000ms) shows it's TRANSIENT. LESSON: for a timing/latency defect, a SINGLE post-broadcast read catches an arbitrary frame — WATCH over several seconds (multi-point) to distinguish transient (vanishes) from persistent (stays). Distrust-your-own-single-read = the timing analog of distrust-your-own-negative.**
- **#3 RECONCILIATION (recap, evidence 79a78f912): endpoint=40.1 CORRECT on LIVE served process in Tron's order (read-after-write + settled); bug(ii) read-after-write staleness RULED OUT; my earlier 'endpoint=none' was a false-read of a retired field. TWO SEPARATE bugs: (A) no-arg getThreeSlots compute-once divergence (scoreboard/pin, accessor fix — latent single-source cleanup, makes #4 impossible, does NOT fix Tron's board); (B) Tron's board = the drawer re-render latency above. Both must land before deploy; the accessor alone does NOT close Tron's screen. Expert building (A)+(B); architect confirmed accessor shape + the (A)-cleanup/(B)-Tron-screen split.**
- **PROBES (evidence): r4057-reconcile-probe.mjs, r4057-park-raw-probe.mjs, r4057-bar-source-probe.mjs.**
- **OPEN: item (5) agreement NOT-GUARANTEED until (B) re-render fix flips it GREEN + my agreement gate re-verified (its cbb7a0906 freshPinRole machinery reads a retired field = needs its own fix so served-green==my-green). item (6) regular-badge liveness PENDING skill-expert 0.2's tickable-task. corpus audit COMPLETE (2 proxy gates neutralized). count/existence sweep COMPLETE (0 proxies).**
